How do I erase my Fitbit device?

Before you give your Fitbit device to another person or return it, erase the device to remove apps, stored data, personal information, credit and debit cards* (for devices with contactless payments), and saved settings.

*Note that erasing your Fitbit device doesn’t remove Suica. If you use Suica, remove Suica in the Google Health app before giving your Fitbit device to another person. For more information, see How do I use Suica on my Fitbit device?

Ace 2
  1. Connect Ace 2 to the charging cable. For instructions, see How do I charge my Fitbit device?
  2. On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app Clear User Data. If you don’t see the option to Clear User Data, close the Settings app and reopen it while Ace 2 is connected to the charger.
  3. When prompted, press the screen for 3 seconds and release. When your tracker vibrates and the clock screen appears, your data is erased.

If you’re giving away your Fitbit device, be sure to remove the device from your account. For instructions, see How do I set up my Fitbit device?

Ace 3, Inspire, Inspire HR, and Inspire 2

To erase your data from your device:

  1. On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app Clear User Data.
  2. When prompted, press the screen for 3 seconds and release. When your tracker vibrates and the clock screen appears, your data is erased.

Note: On Inspire 2, deactivate Tile before you erase your Fitbit device. For instructions, see What should I do if I lose my Fitbit device?

If you’re giving away your Fitbit device, be sure to remove the device from your account. For instructions, see How do I set up my Fitbit device?

Aria series
Aria:
  1. Remove the batteries.
  2. Wait 10 seconds, then reinsert them.
Aria 2:
  1. Remove the batteries.
  2. Press the small button directly above the battery slots.
  3. While pressing the button, reinsert the batteries. Be sure to press the button for at least 10 seconds.
  4. Return the cover to the battery compartment and press down until it’s secure.

If you’re giving away your Fitbit device, be sure to remove the device from your account. For instructions, see How do I set up my Fitbit device?

Note that Aria Air doesn’t store personal data.

On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app AboutClear User Data.

  1. Swipe down from the clock screen to find the Settings app .
  2. On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app Device InfoClear User Data

If you’re giving away your Fitbit device, be sure to remove the device from your account. For instructions, see How do I set up my Fitbit device?

Erase Fitbit Flyer to clear all paired devices from memory and set the language for voice prompts and notifications to English.

Press the following buttons in this order:

  1. Up
  2. Down
  3. Up
  4. Down
  5. Up
  6. Select

When the erase is complete, the LED flashes white 5 times and Flyer turns off.

On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app AboutFactory Reset.

Note: On Fitbit Versa Lite Edition, you can delete apps to remove personal information from your watch. For instructions, see How do I install and manage apps on my Fitbit device?

Other devices

For devices not listed above, your data is automatically cleared when the device is paired to a new account. To remove the Fitbit device from your account, follow the instructions in How do I set up my Fitbit device?

How do I install and manage apps on my Fitbit device?

Your Fitbit device comes preloaded with a variety of apps including Alarms, Weather, and Exercise. On certain devices, install additional apps using the Google Health app.

Apps can be published either publicly or privately. Publicly shared apps appear in the Google Health app search results, and are subject to Google Health review. Privately shared apps, which are typically used for developer testing, can only be found by clicking a direct link to the app. Only install apps from sources you know and trust, and be mindful of what data you share with third parties.

If you have trouble setting up an app, see How do I set up and troubleshoot apps on my Fitbit device?

Following a careful assessment of the impact of new regulatory requirements, we have made the decision to remove the ability to install third-party apps and clocks from the Google Health app for users in the European Economic Area in June 2024. You still have access to a selection of Google Health and Google apps. Users in other countries will not be impacted.

These changes affect the following Fitbit smartwatches:

  • Fitbit Sense 2, Fitbit Sense
  • Fitbit Versa 4, Fitbit Versa 3, Fitbit Versa 2, Fitbit Versa Lite, Fitbit Versa
  • Fitbit Ionic (if you still have a Fitbit Ionic, click here for information on the voluntary recall)
What types of apps are available through the Google Health app?

Find and install new apps for your device in the Google Health app which offers apps to address a variety of health, fitness, timekeeping, and everyday needs. For example, the Weather app allows you to check your local weather on your wrist. Additional apps are added periodically to the Google Health app, so check back often to find new apps. To learn more about developing apps for the Google Health app, see dev.fitbit.com.

Why are some apps labeled as “paid” in the Google Health app?

Some apps and clock faces require a paid subscription service or a one-time activation fee (to a developer). If a developer requires an activation fee, you may see a “Paid” label above the app or clock face description:

For these apps and clock faces, the developer will process and provide instructions for payment. Many developers use the third party service KiezelPay to accept payment for their app or clock face. You’ll see a message on your Fitbit device asking you to visit a website and enter a code. For more information, see KiezelPay’s FAQ. If you decide not to purchase a paid clock face and want to remove the message, change your clock face. For more information, see How do I change the clock face on my Fitbit device?

How do I add apps to my Fitbit device?

Customize Fitbit Charge 4 and Fitbit watches with apps.

On Fitbit Sense, and Fitbit Versa series, note the following:

  • Up to 39 apps can be installed on your watch at a time.
  • Some devices must be connected to Wi-Fi for some apps to install or update. For instructions on connecting your watch to Wi-Fi, see How do I connect my Fitbit watch to Wi-Fi?

To install an app:

  1. Open the Google Health app with your watch nearby.
  2. At the top left, tap Connections and then your device.
  3. Tap Apps tab.
  4. Browse the available apps. When you find the one you want to install, tap it.
  5. Tap Install.
  6. Some apps require certain permissions in order to work properly, such as access to the internet or the ability to run in the background. Review these requested permissions and tap Install to add the app to your device. Note that some permissions can cause your phone to use more data. If you decide at a later time that you don’t want an app to have certain permissions, you can adjust these permissions.
How do I remove apps from my Fitbit watch?

Note that some apps, such as Today and Settings, can’t be removed.

To remove an app:

  1. Open the Google Health app with your watch nearby.
  2. At the top left, tap Connections and then your device.
  3. Tap Apps tab.
  4. In the list of apps installed on your watch, find the app you want to remove and tap it. You may have to swipe up to see some of the apps.
  5. Tap the app and then Uninstall and then Uninstall.
How do I connect my Fitbit account to an app?

To use some apps on your device, you need to create or connect an account with that company’s service. If you don’t already have an account, you may need to visit that company’s website or app in order to create one.

To connect an account with an app you installed:

  1. Open the Google Health app with your watch nearby.
  2. At the top left, tap Connections and then your device.
  3. Tap Apps tab.
  4. In the list of apps installed on your device, tap the app you want to set up. You might have to scroll down to see some apps.
  5. Tap Settings.
  6. Follow the on-screen instructions to log in to your account.
How do I manage apps on my Fitbit watch?
How do I reorder apps on my Fitbit watch?

Apps are shown in groups of 4 per screen on your Fitbit watch.

To change the placement of an app:

  1. On your watch, swipe to the left until you see the app you want to move.
  2. Press and hold the app until it increases slightly in size.
  3. Drag the app to a new location.
How do I update apps on my Fitbit watch?

Watches that connect to Wi-Fi automatically update apps as needed, when plugged into the charger and in range of a saved Wi-Fi network. You can manually check for app updates in the Google Health app at any time. To update apps on Versa Lite, you must manually check for updates in the Google Health app.

To manually check for app updates:

  1. Open the Google Health app with your watch nearby.
  2. At the top left, tap Connections and then your device.
  3. Tap Apps tab.
  4. If an app has a pending update, you’ll see an Update button next to its name. Tap it to update the app.
How do I adjust the settings of apps and clock faces on my Fitbit device?

Many apps include options to adjust the notifications, allow certain permissions, and customize what it displays. Note that turning off any app permissions might cause the app to stop functioning.

To access these settings:

  1. Open the Google Health app with your watch nearby.
  2. At the top left, tap Connections and then your device.
  3. Tap Apps tab or Watch faces.
  4. Tap the app or clock face you want to change. You might have to swipe up to see some apps.
  5. Tap Settings or Permissions.
  6. Tap the back arrow when you’re done making changes.

How do I navigate my Fitbit device?

Learn how to navigate your Fitbit device.

Ace and Alta series

The screen on Fitbit Ace, Fitbit Alta, and Fitbit Alta HR is dimmed when not in use. It wakes up automatically when you turn your wrist towards you (known as Quick View), or you can double-tap your tracker where the display meets the band as shown below.

Screenshot: A tracker on a wrist with a finger tapping the tracker twice

To advance between screens and check your stats, give your device a tap. Note that Ace, Alta, and Alta HR don’t respond to swipes.

Ace 2, Inspire, and Inspire HR

Your tracker has an OLED touchscreen display. Navigate your device by tapping the screen, swiping up and down, or pressing the button:

Use the clock and apps

The home screen is the clock. On the clock:

  • Swipe down to scroll through the apps on your tracker. To open an app, tap it.
  • Swipe up to check your daily stats.

Use the button

Your device has one button.

Quick settings

Press and hold the button to access certain settings. Tap a setting to change it. When you turn off a setting, the icon appears dim with a line through it.

Screenshot: Tracker with the button highlighted and text that says to press and hold the button for 2 seconds

Check battery level

Check your battery level at any time: Press and hold the button. The battery level is at the top.

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings directly on your device. Open the Settings app and then Tap a setting to adjust it. Swipe to check the full list of settings.

Ace 3

Ace 3 has a PMOLED touchscreen display and 2 buttons. Navigate Ace 3 by tapping the screen, swiping up and down, or pressing the buttons:

  • Use the clock and apps
  • Use the buttons
  • Check battery level
  • Adjust settings
  • Turn off the screen

Use the clock and apps

The home screen is the clock.

  • Swipe down to scroll through the apps on your tracker. Tap an app to open it.

  • Swipe up to check your daily stats.

Choose a different clock face in the Google Home app. For more information, check How do I change the clock face on my Fitbit device?

Use the buttons

Ace 3 has 2 buttons. Press and hold the buttons to access quick settings.

Check battery level

To check your battery level, swipe up from the clock face.

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings on your tracker.

Quick settings

Press and hold the buttons to access certain settings. Tap a setting to turn it on or off. When you turn off a setting, the icon appears dim with a line through it.

  • Water Lock
    • Turn on the water lock setting when you’re in water, such as during showering or swimming, to prevent the buttons on your tracker from activating. When the water lock is on, your screen and buttons are locked. Notifications and alarms still appear on your tracker, but you must unlock your screen to interact with them. To turn on water lock, press and hold the buttons on your tracker and then tap Water Lock and then firmly tap your screen twice. To turn off water lock, firmly double-tap the middle of the screen with your finger. If “Unlocked” doesn’t show on screen, try tapping harder. Turning off this feature uses the accelerometer in your device, which takes more force to activate.
  • DND (Do not disturb)
    • When the do not disturb setting is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders are muted. The do not disturb icon illuminates in quick settings. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep
    • When the sleep mode setting is on: Notifications and reminders are muted. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. The screen stays dark when you turn your wrist. Sleep mode turns off automatically when you set a schedule. To set a schedule: Open the Settings app and tap Sleep Mode . Tap Schedule to turn it on. Tap Sleep Interval and set your sleep mode schedule. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When the screen wake setting is on, turn your wrist toward you to turn on the screen.
Settings app

From the clock screen, swipe down and open the Settings app . Tap a setting to adjust it. Swipe up to check the full list of settings.

  • Dim Screen
    • Turn on the dim screen setting to lower your screen’s brightness.
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off.
  • Double Tap
    • Turn on or off the ability to wake your tracker with a firm double tap. Note that your screen must be off for at least 10 seconds for this setting to take effect.
  • Device Info
    • Check your device’s regulatory information and activation date, which is the day your device’s warranty begins. The activation date is the day you set up your device. For more information, check What’s Fitbit’s return and warranty policy?

Turn off the screen

To turn off the screen when not in use, briefly cover the clock face with your opposite hand, press the buttons, or turn your wrist away from your body.

Blaze

Use the clock and menus

The starting screen is the clock. On the clock:

  • Swipe up to check any notifications.
  • Swipe down to use shortcuts for controlling music or turning notifications on and off.
  • Swipe left and right to check menus.
  • (Some clock faces) Tap to check your all-day stats.

To browse different clock designs, check How do I change the clock face on my Fitbit device?

Tap to open a menu:

  • Today: Swipe up to check your steps taken, heart rate, distance covered, calories burned, active minutes, and floors climbed.
  • Exercise: Swipe to find your specific exercise. Refer to How do I track my workouts with my Fitbit device?
  • Fitbit Coach: Choose from 3 Fitstar workouts.
  • Relax: Complete a personalized guided breathing session. Refer to How do I track and manage stress with my Fitbit device?
  • Timer: Swipe to choose a stopwatch or countdown.
  • Alarms: Set or delete silent alarms. For more information Refer to How do I manage alarms on my Fitbit device?
  • Settings: Swipe up to check your options.

Use the buttons

Blaze has 3 buttons: Action, Select, and Back.

Use the Back button to return to the previous screen. The behavior of the Action and Select buttons depends on the screen, but usually these buttons are an alternative to tapping. For example, during an exercise you can tap the pause icon in the lower right or tap the Select button next to it instead.

Charge 2

Use the clock and menus

The default or is a clock. On the clock you can:

  • Tap to check your steps taken, heart rate, distance, calories burned, floors climbed, and active minutes.
  • Press the button to move to specialized menus.

To browse different clock designs, refer to How do I change the clock face on my Fitbit device?

Press the button to open a specialized menu:

  • Heart rate: Shows your current heart rate. Tap to check your resting heart rate.
  • Exercise: Tap to move through your exercise choices, then press and hold the button to start the selected exercise. Press and hold the button again to end the exercise. For more information, check How do I track my workouts with my Fitbit device?
  • Alarms: Tap to scroll through any alarms you’ve set. Press and hold the button to disable or enable any alarms. For more information, check How do I manage alarms on my Fitbit device?
  • Stopwatch: Press and hold the button to start the stopwatch. Press the button to stop and resume the stopwatch. Press and hold the button again to reset the stopwatch.
  • Relax: Tap to choose a guided breathing session. Press and hold the button to start the session.
Charge 3

To get the basics on Fitbit Charge 3, choose a section below.

  • Use the clock and apps
  • Use the button
  • Turn off the screen

Use the clock and apps

The home screen is a clock:

Screenshot: Clock face that shows the time in the middle and heart rate below

On the clock you can:

  • Swipe down to check notifications.
  • Swipe up to check your stats in Google Health.
  • Swipe left to check the apps installed on your device. To open an app, swipe to find the app and then tap it.
  • (Some clock faces) Tap to check your all-day stats.

To browse different clock designs, check How do I change the clock face on my Fitbit device?

Quick settings

Press and hold the button, and swipe left to access the quick settings screen. Tap a setting to turn it on or off. When you turn off a setting, the icon appears dim with a line through it.

  • DND (Do not disturb)
    • When the do not disturb setting is on, notifications, goal celebrations, heart zone alerts, and reminders to move don’t cause the tracker to vibrate or the screen to turn on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep Mode
    • When the sleep mode setting is on: Notifications don’t cause the tracker to vibrate or the screen to turn on. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. Turning your wrist won’t cause the tracker’s screen to turn on. Sleep mode doesn’t automatically turn off unless you set a sleep mode schedule. To schedule sleep mode to automatically turn on during certain hours: Open the Settings app and tap Sleep Mode . Tap Schedule to turn it on. Tap Sleep Interval and set your sleep mode schedule. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When the screen wake setting is on, turn your wrist toward you to turn on the screen.
Settings app

Open the Settings app and swipe up to check the full list of settings. Tap a setting to adjust it.

  • Brightness
    • Change the screen’s brightness.
  • Vibrations
    • Adjust your tracker’s vibration strength.
  • Heart Rate
    • Turn heart-rate tracking on or off.
  • Do Not Disturb
    • Turn off all notifications. Choose to receive alerts about your workout in the Exercise app even if Do Not Disturb is on.
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off.
  • Screen Time Out
    • Choose how long your screen stays on before it turns off.

To turn off the screen when not in use, push the button.

  • DND (Do not disturb)
    • When the do not disturb setting is on, notifications, goal celebrations, heart zone alerts, and reminders to move don’t cause the tracker to vibrate or the screen to turn on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep Mode
    • When the sleep mode setting is on: Notifications don’t cause the tracker to vibrate or the screen to turn on. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. Turning your wrist won’t cause the tracker’s screen to turn on. Sleep mode doesn’t automatically turn off unless you set a sleep mode schedule. To schedule sleep mode to automatically turn on during certain hours: Open the Settings app and tap Sleep Mode . Tap Schedule to turn it on. Tap Sleep Interval and set your sleep mode schedule. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When the screen wake setting is on, turn your wrist toward you to turn on the screen.
Settings app

Open the Settings app and swipe up to check the full list of settings. Tap a setting to adjust it.

  • Brightness
    • Change the screen’s brightness.
  • Vibrations
    • Adjust your tracker’s vibration strength.
  • Screen Timeout
    • Choose how long the screen stays on after you stop interacting with your tracker.
  • Heart Rate
    • Turn heart-rate tracking on or off.
  • Do Not Disturb
    • Turn off all notifications. Choose to receive alerts about your workout in the Exercise app even if Do Not Disturb is on.
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off.
  • Heart Zone Minutes Notifications
    • Turn on or off the Active zone Minutes Notifications you receive during everyday activity. For more information, check What are Active Zone Minutes or active minutes on my Fitbit device?
  • GPS Settings
    • Choose the GPS type and manage other settings to enhance GPS performance. For more information, check How do I use GPS on my Fitbit device?
  • Device Info
    • Check your device’s regulatory information and activation date, which is the day your device’s warranty begins. The activation date is the day you set up your device. For more information, check What’s Fitbit’s return and warranty policy?

Turn off the screen

To turn off the screen when not in use, briefly cover the clock face with your opposite hand, press the button, or turn your wrist away from your body.

Screenshot: The face of a smartwatch covered by a person’s palm

Charge 5 and Luxe

Charge 5 and Luxe have a color AMOLED touchscreen display. Navigate your tracker by tapping the screen and swiping side to side and up and down.

  • Use the clock and apps
  • Adjust settings
  • Check battery level
  • Turn off the screen

Use the clock and apps

    • Wake your device Firmly tap the screen twice or turn your wrist to wake your tracker.
    • Access settings Swipe down from the top of the screen to access quick settings, the Settings app, the Payments app (Charge 5 only), and the Find Phone app. While in quick settings, swipe right to return to the clock screen.
    • Open apps Swipe left and right to check the apps on your tracker. Swipe up or tap the app to open it.
    • Check your stats Swipe up to check your daily stats. To return to the clock screen, swipe right.
    • Go back Swipe right to go back to a previous screen. Firmly double-tap to return to the clock screen. To return to the clock screen from quick settings or your daily stats, swipe right.

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings on your tracker.

Quick settings

For a faster way to access certain settings, swipe down from the clock screen. Tap a setting to adjust it. When you turn off a setting, the icon appears dim with a line through it.

  • DND mode
    • When the do not disturb setting is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders are muted. The do not disturb icon illuminates in quick settings. You can’t turn on the do not disturb setting and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep mode
    • When sleep mode is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders are muted. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. The screen stays dark when you turn your wrist. To wake your tracker, firmly tap the screen twice. The sleep mode icon illuminates in quick settings. Sleep mode turns off automatically when you set a sleep schedule. For more information, check the . You can’t turn on the do not disturb setting and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen wake
    • When you set screen wake to Auto , the screen turns on each time you turn your wrist. When you set screen wake to Manual , firmly tap the screen twice to turn it on.
  • Find Phone
    • Tap to open the Find Phone app . For more information, check How do I set up and troubleshoot apps on my Fitbit device?
  • Water lock
    • Turn on the water lock setting when you’re in water, such as during showering or swimming, to prevent your screen from activating. When the water lock is on, your screen locks, and the water lock icon appears at the bottom. Notifications and alarms still appear on your tracker, but you must unlock your screen to interact with them. To turn on water lock, swipe down from the clock screen and then tap Water Lock and then firmly tap your screen twice. To turn off water lock, firmly double-tap the middle of the screen with your finger. If “Unlocked” doesn’t appear on screen, try tapping harder. Turning off this feature uses the accelerometer in your device, which takes more force to activate. Note that water lock turns on automatically when you start a swim in the Exercise app.
  • Settings app
    • Tap to open the Settings app . For more information, check .
Settings app

For a full list of settings, swipe down from the clock screen and open the Settings app . Swipe up to check the full list of settings, and tap a setting to adjust it.

Display
  • Brightness
    • Change the screen’s brightness.
  • Screen Wake
    • Change whether the screen turns on when you turn your wrist.
  • Screen Time Out
    • Adjust the amount of time before the screen turns off.
  • Always-on display
    • Turn the always-on display on or off. For more information, check How do I see the time on my Fitbit device?
Quiet modes
  • Exercise Focus
    • Turn off notifications while using the Exercise app .
  • Do Not Disturb
    • Turn off all notifications.
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off. To set a schedule: Open the Settings app and tap Quiet Modes and then Sleep Mode and then Schedule and then Interval . Tap the start or end time to adjust when the mode turns on and off. Swipe up or down to change the time, and tap the time to select it. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on.
Other settings
  • Heart Rate
    • Turn heart-rate tracking on or off.
  • Heart Zone Notifications
    • Turn heart-zone notifications on or off. For more information, refer to What are Active Zone Minutes or active minutes on my Fitbit device?
  • Vibrations
    • Adjust your tracker’s vibration strength.
  • GPS (Charge 5 only)
    • Choose the GPS mode. For more information, refer to How do I use GPS on my Fitbit device?
  • Device info
    • Check your device’s regulatory information and activation date, which is the day your device’s warranty begins. The activation date is the day you set up your device. Tap Device Info and then System Info to check the activation date. For more information, refer to What’s Fitbit’s return and warranty policy?
  • Restart device
    • Tap Restart device and then Restart to restart your tracker.

Understand status indicators

When you turn on your device’s screen, icons at the top of the clock face appear:

Screenshot: Status indicators at the top of the clock screen

    • Your Fitbit device’s do not disturb setting is turned on. For more information, check Quick settings .
    • Your Fitbit device’s sleep mode setting is turned on. For more information, check Quick settings .
    • Your Fitbit device’s battery is critically low. For more information, check How do I charge my Fitbit device?
    • Your Fitbit device isn’t connected to your phone. For troubleshooting tips, check Why won’t my Fitbit device sync?

The icons disappear after 3 seconds. To show them again, swipe right to check the icons at the top of quick settings.

To turn status indicators on or off:

  1. On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app and then Display.
  2. Tap the switch next to Status indicators.

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings on your tracker.

Quick settings

Press and hold the buttons to access certain settings. Tap a setting to turn it on or off. When you turn off a setting, the icon appears dim with a line through it.

  • Water Lock
    • Turn on the water lock setting when you’re in water, such as during showering or swimming, to prevent the buttons on your tracker from activating. When water lock is on, your screen and buttons are locked. Notifications and alarms still appear on your tracker, but you must unlock your screen to interact with them. To turn on water lock, press and hold the buttons on your tracker and then tap Water Lock and then firmly tap your screen twice. To turn off water lock, firmly double-tap the middle of the screen with your finger. If the message “Unlocked” doesn’t appear on screen, try tapping harder. Turning off this feature uses the accelerometer in your device, which takes more force to activate. Note that water lock turns on automatically when you start a swim in the Exercise app .
  • DND (Do not disturb)
    • When the do not disturb setting is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders are muted. The do not disturb icon illuminates in quick settings. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep
    • When the sleep mode setting is on: Notifications and reminders are muted. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. The screen stays dark when you turn your wrist. Sleep mode turns off automatically when you set a schedule. To set a schedule: Open the Settings app and tap Sleep Mode . Tap Schedule to turn it on. Tap Sleep Interval and set your sleep mode schedule. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When the screen wake setting is on, turn your wrist toward you to turn on the screen.
  • On Wrist or On Clip setting
    • When Inspire 2 is on your wrist, make sure your tracker is set to On Wrist . When Inspire 2 is in the accessory clip, make sure it’s set to On Clip . To switch between On Wrist and On Clip mode, tap Settings and then tap the checkmark to confirm. Note: When Inspire 2 is set to On Clip , certain features are turned off, including heart-rate tracking, automatic exercise tracking, and Active Zone Minutes.
Settings app

From the clock screen, swipe down and open the Settings app . Tap a setting to adjust it. Swipe up to check the full list of settings.

  • Dim Screen
    • Turn on the dim screen setting to lower your screen’s brightness.
  • Phone GPS
    • Turn connected GPS on or off. For more information, refer to How do I use GPS on my Fitbit device?
  • Heart Rate
    • Turn heart rate tracking on or off. Note that this setting is only available when Inspire 2 is set to On Wrist .
  • Do Not Disturb
    • Turn off all notifications or choose to automatically turn on Do not disturb while using the Exercise app .
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off.
  • Heart Zone Notifications
    • Turn heart zone notifications on or off. For more information, refer to What are Active Zone Minutes or active minutes on my Fitbit device?
  • Double Tap
    • Turn on or off the ability to wake your tracker with a firm double tap. Note that your screen must be off for at least 10 seconds for this setting to take effect.
  • Button Lock
    • Turn on Button Lock to prevent the buttons on your tracker from activating while your screen is off. To turn Button Lock on or off, tap Settings and then tap the checkmark to confirm. Tip: Use this setting to avoid unintended button presses while you exercise, especially if you wear your tracker lower on your wrist or you tighten the band to be extra snug. For more information, refer to How do I wear my Fitbit device? Note: Using the Button Lock setting on Inspire 2 may require more frequent charging.
  • Device Info
    • Check your device’s regulatory information and activation date, which is the day your device’s warranty begins. The activation date is the day you set up your device. For more information, refer to What’s Fitbit’s return and warranty policy?

Turn off the screen

To turn off the screen when not in use, briefly cover the clock face with your opposite hand, press the buttons, or turn your wrist away from your body.

Use the button shortcuts

Left button

Press and hold the left button for 2 seconds to quickly access a feature such as Alexa, music controls, notifications, or certain apps. To change which feature activates when you hold the button, open the Settings app and tap the Left Button option. The possible shortcuts vary based on your device.

Top and bottom buttons (Versa only)

From the clock screen, press the top and bottom buttons on the right side of the watch to quickly access your favorite apps.

  • Top button: Opens the app located in the top left of the first app screen.
  • Bottom button: opens the app located in the bottom left of the first app screen.

Customize your watch by reordering your apps. For more information, refer to How do I install and manage apps on my Fitbit device?

    • Control music playing on your phone or watch.

    • Adjust certain settings such as screen brightness and screen wake. For more information, check .

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings on your watch.

Quick settings

Adjust notification and display settings. Swipe down from the top of your screen. After your notifications appear, swipe down again to open the control center. Tap the quick settings icon .

  • Do Not Disturb
    • When you turn on the do not disturb setting: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders to move don’t cause the watch to vibrate or the screen to turn on. You get a do not disturb icon at the top of the screen when you swipe down to check your notifications. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep Mode
    • When you turn on the sleep mode setting: Notifications don’t cause the watch to vibrate or the screen to turn on. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. The Always-On Display clock face is turned off (on watches that support it). Turning your wrist won’t cause the watch’s screen to turn on. You get a sleep mode icon at the top of the screen when you swipe down to check your notifications. To schedule sleep mode to automatically turn on during certain hours, open the Settings app and tap Sleep Mode and then Schedule . Sleep Mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When you set Screen Wake to Auto (Motion), the screen turns on each time you turn your wrist. When you set Screen Wake to Manual (Button), press the button to turn on the screen. You can’t set Screen Wake to Auto (Motion) if you turn on always-on display or sleep mode.
  • Brightness
    • Adjust the screen brightness.
  • Always-On Display (Versa 2 only)
    • Keep the screen on, even when you’re not interacting with the watch. For more information, check How do I see the time on my Fitbit device? Note that turning on this feature impacts your watch’s battery. When the always-on display is turned on, Versa 2 has a battery life of up to 3 days.
Settings app

For a full list of settings, open the Settings app . Swipe up to check the full list of settings, and tap a setting to adjust it.

  • Brightness
    • Change the screen’s brightness.
  • Screen Timeout
    • Choose how long the screen stays on after you stop interacting with your watch.
  • Always-On Display (Versa 2 only)
    • Adjust always-on display settings, including the information you get on the clock face. Note that turning on this feature impacts your watch’s battery. When the always-on display is turned on, Versa 2 has a battery life of up to 3 days.
  • Sleep Mode
    • Adjust sleep mode settings, including setting a schedule for the mode to automatically turn on and off.
  • Do Not Disturb
    • Adjust do not disturb settings, including whether the mode should turn on automatically when you use the Exercise app.
  • Screen Wake
    • Change whether the screen turns on when you turn your wrist.
  • Vibrations
    • Adjust your watch’s vibration strength.

    • Choose whether your watch can access the microphone.

    • Manage connected Bluetooth devices.

    • Turn heart-rate tracking on or off.

    • Turn Active Zone Minutes weekly goal notifications on or off.

    • Check your device’s regulatory information and activation date, which is the day your device’s warranty begins. The activation date is the day you set up your device. Use button shortcuts

How do I use the button on my Fitbit Sense or Fitbit Versa 3?

To use the button on your watch, press the entire button sensor, where the indentation is. You’ll feel your watch vibrate. You may have to experiment to find the best position.

Screenshot: Illustration of a watch with text indicating you should hold the entire button for two seconds

You might unintentionally press the button on your watch when you bend your wrist. This may happen during activities such as a workout that require you to move your wrist a lot. Try wearing your watch higher on your wrist to avoid hitting the button. For tips on how to wear your watch, check How do I wear my Fitbit device?

Press and hold the button

Press and hold the left button for 2 seconds to quickly access a feature such as Alexa, Google Assistant, music controls, notifications, or certain apps. To change which feature activates when you hold the button, open the Settings app on your watch and tap Shortcuts. In the Press & hold section, tap Press & hold. Choose your shortcut, and then swipe right to return to the previous screen.

Double-press the button

Double-press the button to open shortcuts to apps or features. To start, the shortcuts are music controls , quick settings , your voice assistant. To change these shortcuts, open the Settings app on your watch and tap Shortcuts. In the Double Press section, tap the shortcut you want to change. Swipe right to return to the previous screen.

Adjust settings

Manage basic settings on your watch.

Quick settings

Swipe right from the clock face on your watch to access the quick settings.

  • Do Not Disturb
    • When the do not disturb setting is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders to move are muted. The do not disturb icon illuminates in the quick settings. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Sleep Mode
    • When the sleep mode setting is on: Notifications, goal celebrations, and reminders to move are muted. The screen’s brightness is set to dim. The Always-On Display clock face is turned off. The screen stays dark when you turn your wrist. The sleep mode icon illuminates in the quick settings. Sleep mode turns off automatically when you set a sleep schedule. To set a schedule: Open the Settings app and tap Quiet modes . Under Sleep mode, tap Schedule mode and then Off-hours . Tap the start or stop time to adjust when the mode turns on and off. Swipe up or down to change the time, and tap the time to select it. Sleep mode automatically turns off at the time you schedule, even if you manually turned it on. You can’t turn on do not disturb and sleep mode at the same time.
  • Screen Wake
    • When you set screen wake to automatic , the screen turns on each time you turn your wrist. When you set screen wake to manual, press the button or tap the screen to turn on the display.
  • Brightness
    • Adjust the screen brightness.
  • Always-On Display
    • Turn always-on display on or off. For more information, check How do I see the time on my Fitbit device?
  • Music Volume
    • Adjust the volume of music playing through headphones or speakers paired to your watch.

Understand status indicators

When you turn on your device’s screen, icons at the top of the clock face appear:

Screenshot: Status indicators at the top of the clock screen

    • Your Fitbit device’s do not disturb setting is turned on. For more information, check Quick settings .
    • Your Fitbit device’s sleep mode setting is turned on. For more information, check Quick settings .
    • Your Fitbit device’s battery is critically low. For more information, check How do I charge my Fitbit device?
    • Your Fitbit device isn’t connected to your phone. For troubleshooting tips, check Why won’t my Fitbit device sync?

The icons disappear after 3 seconds. To show them again, swipe right to check the icons at the top of quick settings.

To turn status indicators on or off:

  1. On your Fitbit device, open the Settings app and then Display.
  2. Tap the switch next to Status indicators.

Check widgets

Add widgets to your watch to check your daily stats, log your water intake or weight, check the weather forecast, and access certain apps. To check your widgets, swipe up from the clock face.

Screenshot: Widgets that show the weather forecast in several locations and the user’s core stats

To add a new widget:

  1. From the clock face, swipe up to the bottom of the widgets, and tap Manage.
  2. Under More Widgets, tap next to the widget you want to add.
  3. Swipe up to the bottom of the page, and tap Done.

To turn off a widget:

  1. From the clock face, swipe up to the bottom of the widgets, and tap Manage.
  2. Tap the widget you want to adjust.
  3. Tap the switch next to Show Widget to turn it off.
  4. Swipe up to the bottom of the page, and tap Done.

To adjust the information you get on a widget:

  1. From the clock face, swipe up to the bottom of the widgets, and tap Manage.
  2. Tap and then next to the widget you want to adjust.
  3. Adjust any settings you want to change.
  4. Swipe up to the bottom of the page, and tap Done.

To change the order of widgets:

  1. From the clock face, swipe up to the bottom of the widgets, and tap Manage.
  2. Press and hold the widget you want to move, and drag it up or down in the list of widgets. When it’s in the correct new location, lift your finger.
  3. Swipe up to the bottom of the page, and tap Done.

Can I keep the clock display on?

To keep the clock visible at all times, turn on the always-on display setting.

Always-on display impacts your device’s battery. When always-on display is turned on, your device requires more frequent charging.

Charge 5, Charge 6, Inspire 3, and Luxe
  1. Swipe down from the clock face to access quick settings.
  2. Tap the Settings app and then Display settings and then Always-on display.
  3. Tap Always-on display to turn the setting on or off. To set the display to automatically turn off during certain hours, tap Off-hours.

Clock faces without an always-on display mode use a default always-on display clock face.

Always-on display automatically turns off when your tracker’s battery is critically low.

Sense and Versa 3
  1. Swipe right from the clock face to open your quick settings.
  2. Tap the always-on display icon .

Clock faces without an always-on display mode use a default always-on display clock face. Choose an analog or digital clock face. Open the Settings app and then Display. In the Always-on display section, tap Analog or Digital.

Always-on display automatically turns off when your watch’s battery is critically low.

To exit the always-on display and interact with your watch, turn your wrist toward you, press the button, or double-tap the display.

Sense 2 and Versa 4
  1. Swipe down from the clock face to open your quick settings.
  2. Tap the always-on display icon .

Always-on display automatically turns off when your watch’s battery is critically low.

To exit the always-on display and interact with your watch, turn your wrist toward you, press the button, or double-tap the display.

Versa 2
  1. Swipe down from the top of your screen. After your notifications appear, swipe down again to open the control center. Tap the quick settings icon .
  2. In the quick settings screen, tap the always-on display icon .
  3. To set off-hours when the always-on display automatically turns off, open the Settings app and then Always-On Display and then Customize.

Many clock faces have their own always-on display mode. If a clock face doesn’t have this feature, you see the default always-on display clock face. To adjust what’s shown on the default always-on display clock, open the Settings app and then Always-On Display and then Customize.

How do I turn off my Fitbit device?

You can turn off certain Fitbit devices to preserve battery life. If you don’t see your device listed below, it doesn’t turn off.

For battery preservation tips, see Can I extend my Fitbit device’s battery life?

Note that shutting down your device doesn’t delete any data.

Blaze
  1. From the home screen, swipe left to the Settings.
  2. Tap Settings and then Shutdown and confirm.
  3. To turn Blaze back on, press any button.
  4. Sync your tracker as soon as you turn it back on. If you don’t, the time may be incorrect.
Flyer
  1. Press and hold the Power button on the right earbud for 1 second until the LED changes color.
  2. Listen for a tone and audio notification. If you turned on Flyer, you’ll hear the current battery level and number of connected devices. If you turned off Flyer, you’ll hear the current battery level. Flyer turns off automatically if you haven’t played music or received a phone call for 10 minutes.

If Flyer doesn’t turn on when you press and hold the Power button, charge your headphones. For more information about charging Flyer, see How do I charge my Fitbit device?

If Flyer is turned on, but isn’t connected to a device, go to Fitbit User Manuals.

Versa, Versa Lite Edition, and Versa 2
  1. On your watch, open the Settings app and then About and then Shutdown and then confirm the shutdown.
  2. To turn your watch back on, press the back button.
Sense series, Versa 3, and Versa 4
  1. On your watch, open the Settings app and then Shut down and then confirm the shutdown.
  2. To turn your watch back on, press and hold the button until you feel your watch vibrate.

Restart your Fitbit device

Restart your Fitbit device to troubleshoot the following issues:

  • Your device won’t sync with the Google Health app
  • Your device is unresponsive to taps, swipes, or button presses
  • Your device’s display is blank or frozen
  • Your device doesn’t register steps or other data
  • Your device doesn’t show notifications or reminders
  • Your device is in a continuous reboot loop

A restart turns your device off and on without the loss of any activity data.

Important: Some Fitbit devices have small holes for sensors such as the microphone or altimeter. Don’t try to restart your device by inserting any items, such as paper clips, into these holes as this can damage your Fitbit device.

Google Fitbit Air

Google Fitbit Air

Screenshot: Reset Google Fitbit Air

  1. Plug the charging cable into a USB-C power source.
  2. Put Google Fitbit Air on the charger.
  3. While keeping your device on the charger, press and hold the button on the back of the charger for 10 seconds, or until the status light on the side of Google Fitbit Air flashes white.
  4. Let go of the button. Your device has restarted.

Fitbit Charge series

Charge 5 and Charge 6
  1. Swipe down from the clock screen to find the Settings app .
  2. Tap the Settings app and then Restart Device and then Restart.

If your device is unresponsive:

  1. Connect your device to the charging cable. For instructions, refer to How do I charge my Fitbit device?
  2. Press the button located on the flat end of the charger 3 times with one-second pause between each press. Then wait 10 seconds or until the Fitbit logo appears.

Another way to restart your device is to unplug it, wait a second, and plug it back in. Repeat this process 3 times in an 8 seconds interval and then wait 10 seconds until the device restarts.

Charge 3 and Charge 4

Open the Settings app on your device and tap About and then Reboot Device.

If your tracker is unresponsive:

  1. Plug the charging cable into the USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Clip the other end of the charging cable to the port on the back of the tracker. The pins on the charging cable must lock securely with the port.
  3. Make sure the button on your tracker is aligned with the button opening on the charging cable. You’ll know the connection is secure when the tracker vibrates and you notice a battery icon on your tracker’s display.
  4. Press and hold the button on your tracker for 8 seconds. Release the button. When the tracker vibrates and shows a smile icon, the tracker has restarted.
Charge 2
  1. Plug the charging cable into the USB port on your computer or a UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Clip the other end of the charging cable to the port on the back of the tracker. The pins on the charging cable must lock securely with the port.
  3. Make sure the button on your tracker is aligned with the button opening on the charging cable. You’ll know the connection is secure when the tracker vibrates and displays a battery icon. Your Charge 2 will begin charging.
  4. Press and hold the button on your tracker for 4 seconds. When the Fitbit logo appears and the tracker vibrates, your device has restarted.
Charge and Charge HR
Restart
  1. Plug the charging cable into the USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Insert the other end into the port on the back of your tracker.
  3. Press and hold the button for 10 to 12 seconds, until it displays the Fitbit icon and a version number (e.g. “V30”).
  4. Let go of the button, and unplug your tracker from the charging cable.
Factory Reset

Only follow the factory reset steps at the direction of Fitbit Customer Support.

A factory reset deletes any stored data, which includes all data that hasn’t synced to your Google Health account and your alarms, goals, and notifications. After a factory reset, you must set up your tracker again.

  1. Attach the charging cable to your tracker and plug the other end into a USB port.
  2. Press and hold the button for approximately 2 seconds and, with the button still pressed down:
    • Disconnect the charging cable from your tracker.
    • Wait about 7 to 9 seconds.
  3. Release the button and hold it down again.
  4. After “ALT” appears on the screen and it flashes white, release the button and hold it down again.
  5. After you feel a vibration, let go of the button and hold it down again.
  6. When “ERROR 0X01” appears on the screen, release the button and hold it down again.
  7. When “ERASE” appears on the screen, release the button. The tracker will turn off.
  8. To turn your tracker back on, press the button or connect it to the charging cable. If the time on your tracker reads 0:00, the reset is successful.
  9. Set up your tracker as a new device. For instructions, refer to How do I set up my Fitbit device?

Inspire 3
  1. Swipe down from the clock screen to find the Settings app and then tap the app to open it.
  2. Swipe up and tap Restart Device.

  1. Connect your device to the charging cable. For instructions, refer to How do I charge my Fitbit device?
  2. Press and hold the buttons on your tracker for 10 seconds.
  3. Release the buttons. The tracker has restarted when it vibrates and the Fitbit logo shows up.

  3. Press and hold the buttons on your tracker for 10 seconds.
  4. Release the buttons. When the tracker vibrates and shows a smile icon, the tracker has restarted.

  3. Press and hold the button or buttons on your tracker for 5 seconds.
  4. Release the button. When the tracker vibrates and shows a smile icon, your tracker has restarted.

Other devices

Ace
  1. Plug the charging cable into a USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Clip the other end of the charging cable to the port on the back of the tracker. The pins on the charging cable should lock securely with the port. You know the connection is secure when the tracker vibrates and you notice a battery icon on the display. Your tracker will begin charging.
  3. Press the button on your charging cable 3 times within a few seconds, with a short pause between each click. The button is on the end of the charging cable that connects to your computer. When the Fitbit logo appears and the tracker vibrates, your tracker has restarted.
  4. Unplug your tracker from the charging cable.
Ace 2 and Ace 3
  1. Plug the charging cable into the USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Connect your device to the charging cable. For instructions, check How do I charge my Fitbit device?
  3. Press and hold the button or buttons on your tracker for 5 seconds.
  4. Release the button. When the tracker vibrates and shows a smile icon, your tracker has restarted.
Alta and Alta HR
  1. Plug the charging cable into a USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Clip the other end of the charging cable to the port on the back of the tracker. The pins on the charging cable should lock securely with the port. You know the connection is secure when the tracker vibrates and you notice a battery icon on the display. Your tracker will begin charging.
  3. Press the button on your charging cable 3 times within a few seconds, with a short pause between each click. The button is on the end of the charging cable that connects to your computer. When the Fitbit logo appears and the tracker vibrates, your tracker has restarted.
  4. Unplug your tracker from the charging cable.
Blaze
  1. Press and hold the back and bottom buttons until you notice the Fitbit logo on the screen.
  2. Let go of the buttons.
  3. If you still have issues with your device, turn it off and turn it back on. For more information, check out How do I turn off my Fitbit device?
Flex and Flex 2
  1. Plug your charging cable into a USB port on your computer or any UL-certified USB wall charger.
  2. Remove the pebble from the band and insert it into the charging cable.
  3. Insert a paperclip into the small pinhole on the back of the charger.
  4. Press on the pinhole for 3 to 4 seconds.
  5. Take your tracker out of the charger and insert it back into the band.
Luxe
  1. Swipe down from the clock screen to find the Settings app .
  2. Tap the Settings app and then Restart Device and then Restart.

If your device is unresponsive:

  1. Connect your device to the charging cable. For instructions, refer to How do I charge my Fitbit device?
  2. Press the button located on the flat end of the charger 3 times with one-second pause between each press. Then wait 10 seconds or until the Fitbit logo appears.

You can also restart your device in another way. Disconnect the device from the charging cable, wait one second, then reconnect. Repeat this process 3 times within an 8-second interval. After that, wait 10 seconds for the device to restart.

One

  1. Plug your charging cable into your computer.

  2. Plug Fitbit One into the charging cable.

    Tip: Make sure that the gold contacts on the tracker align with the gold contacts on the charger.

  3. Hold the button on your tracker for 12 seconds.

  4. Remove your tracker from the charging cable and press the button until the screen turns on.

Surge
  1. Press and hold the Home and Select buttons (left and bottom right) on Surge until the screen flashes or starts to dim (about 15 seconds).
  2. Let go of the buttons.
  3. After your screen turns completely off, wait 10 seconds and press the Home button to turn Surge back on.
Zip
  1. Tap the battery door with the battery door tool and remove the battery.
  2. Wait 10 seconds.
  3. Place the battery back into the tracker with the plus icon + side up, and close the door. You’ll notice your tracker’s version number, followed by a series of smiley faces.

Tip: If you can’t restart your device or if this doesn’t resolve the issue, reach out to Customer Support.

Setup

Charge your Fitbit device

Your Fitbit device comes with a rechargeable lithium-ion battery and a USB charging cable unique to your device’s model.

To charge your device:

  1. Plug the charging cable into:
    • A USB port on your computer
    • A safety-certified USB wall charger
    • Another safety-certified, low-energy power source
  2. Attach the other end of the cable to your device.

Tip: Charging your device to 100% may take one to two hours.

For troubleshooting steps, go to Why isn’t my Fitbit device’s battery charging?

Attach the charging cable to your Fitbit device
Google Fitbit Air
  1. Hold the end of the charging cable near the back of Google Fitbit Air until it attaches magnetically.
  2. The connection is secure when the tracker vibrates and the status light on the side of the tracker lights up.

When Google Fitbit Air has completed charging, the status light will show a solid white light.

Screenshot: Put Google Fitbit Air on the charger

Charge 6

Hold the charging cable near the port on the back of the tracker until it attaches magnetically. Make sure the pins on the charging cable align with the port on the back of your tracker. The charge percentage appears on the screen.

Tip: Charge 5, Charge 6, and Luxe use the same charging cable.

Inspire 3
  1. Align the pins on the end of the charging cable with the gold contacts on the back of your device.
  2. Gently press your device into the charger until it snaps into place.

To remove your device from the charging cable:

  1. Hold the device and slide your thumbnail between the charger and the back of the tracker on one of the corners.
  2. Gently press down on the charger until it separates from your device.
Sense 2 and Versa 4

Hold the end of the charging cable near the port on the back of the watch until it attaches magnetically. The charge percentage appears on screen.

Check the battery level on your Fitbit device

Open the Google Health app . On the Today tab , tap Connections . The battery level appears to the right of your device. You can also check the battery level on most devices.

Google Fitbit Air

Double-tap your device to activate the status light. The color shows your estimated battery level..

  • White light: More than 20%
  • Red light: Less than 20%

When the battery is low, the device displays a red light and vibrates.

  • Slowly-blinking red light: Your device has less than 20% battery left.
  • Solid red light: Your device has run out of battery.
    • If your device is completely out of battery, it can also show no light at all.

Screenshot: Low battery status light on Google Fitbit AirTip: Google Fitbit Air charges from 0 to 100% in about 90 minutes.
